## Calculate Margin

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/margincalculator

Fetch span, exposure, var, brokerage, leverage, and available margin values for any type of order and instrument that you want to place.

See the **SPAN**, exposure and total margin, brokerage and leverage an order needs, before you place it.

**Before you start with Calculate Margin API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.
3. Enter the order you want to check.

## Sample request for Calculate Margin API

Margin for buying 1 share of Vodafone Idea (`securityId` 14366) at ₹12.97 for delivery. Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/margincalculator \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "dhanClientId": "<your-client-id>",
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "transactionType": "BUY",
    "quantity": 1,
    "productType": "CNC",
    "securityId": "14366",
    "price": 12.97,
    "triggerPrice": 0
  }'
```


## Sample response for Calculate Margin API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "totalMargin": 12.97,
  "spanMargin": 0.0,
  "exposureMargin": 0.0,
  "availableBalance": 25000.0,
  "variableMargin": 12.97,
  "insufficientBalance": 0.0,
  "brokerage": 0.0,
  "leverage": "1.00"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | Yes | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| exchangeSegment | enum | Yes | Exchange Segment (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_EQ`, `NSE_FNO`, `NSE_COMM`, `BSE_EQ`, `BSE_FNO`, `MCX_COMM`. |
| transactionType | enum | Yes | The trading side of transaction Values: `BUY`, `SELL`. |
| quantity | int | Yes | Number of shares for the order |
| productType | enum | Yes | Product type of trade (see Annexure) Values: `CNC`, `INTRADAY`, `MARGIN`, `MTF`. |
| securityId | string | Yes | Exchange standard ID for each scrip. refer here |
| price | float | No | Price at which order is placed |
| triggerPrice | float | No | Price at which order is triggered |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| totalMargin | float | No | Overall combined Margin required |
| spanMargin | float | No | Maximum possible loss that the portfolio might face |
| exposureMargin | float | No | Value represents in excess of the SPAN margin |
| availableBalance | float | No | Represents overall usable balance in the account |
| variableMargin | float | No | Variable margin |
| insufficientBalance | float | No | Insufficient balance |
| brokerage | float | No | Brokerage |
| leverage | string | No | Leverage |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Margin calculation |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/margincalculator \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "dhanClientId": "your-client-id",
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "transactionType": "BUY",
    "quantity": 1,
    "productType": "CNC",
    "securityId": "1333",
    "price": 500.00
  }'
```

## Calculate Multi-Order Margin

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/margincalculator/multi

Calculate combined margin for multiple orders with hedge benefits.

The Multi Order Margin Calculator API allows users to calculate margin requirements for multiple scripts in a single request, including span, exposure, equity, F&O, and commodity margins.

Note: Margin values returned are indicative and valid only for the current trading session.

**Before you start with Calculate Multi-Order Margin API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.
3. Add each order to `scripList`.

## Sample request for Calculate Multi-Order Margin API

Margin for buying 1 share of Vodafone Idea (`securityId` 14366) at ₹12.97. Add more objects to `scripList` to include more orders. Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/margincalculator/multi \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "dhanClientId": "<your-client-id>",
    "includePosition": "false",
    "includeOrder": "false",
    "scripList": [
      {
        "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
        "transactionType": "BUY",
        "quantity": 1,
        "productType": "CNC",
        "securityId": "14366",
        "price": 12.97,
        "triggerPrice": 0
      }
    ]
  }'
```


## Sample response for Calculate Multi-Order Margin API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "clientId": "1000000132",
  "totalMargin": 12.97,
  "spanMargin": 0.0,
  "exposure": 0.0,
  "equityMargin": 12.97,
  "foMargin": 0.0,
  "commodity": 0.0,
  "currency": 0.0
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.

## Cancel Order

`DELETE` https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/{order-id}

Cancel a pending order.

The response returns the `orderId` and its `orderStatus`. Confirm the cancellation with [Get Order by ID](/api/v2/orders/get-order-by-id) or the [Live Order Update](/api/v2/order-update) feed.

**Before you start with Cancel Order API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Whitelist your [static IP](/api/v2/authentication/set-ip). Orders only go through from it.
3. Copy the `orderId` of a pending order from the [order book](/api/v2/orders/get-orders) and enter it under Path Parameters below.

> **NOTICE:** Order Placement, Modification and Cancellation APIs requires Static IP whitelisting - [refer here](/api/v2/authentication#setup-static-ip)

## Sample request for Cancel Order API

Cancel a pending order. There is no request body. Replace the order ID (`112111182198` here) and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request DELETE \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/112111182198 \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for Cancel Order API

```json title="202 Accepted"
{
  "orderId": "112111182198",
  "orderStatus": "CANCELLED"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Path Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| order-id | string | Yes | Order ID of the Order being cancelled |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| orderId | string | No | Order specific identification generated by Dhan |
| orderStatus | enum string | No | Last updated status of the order Values: `TRANSIT`, `PENDING`, `REJECTED`, `CANCELLED`, `TRADED`. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Order cancelled |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X DELETE https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/{order-id} \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Cancel Super Order Leg

`DELETE` https://api.dhan.co/v2/super/orders/{order-id}/{order-leg}

Cancel a specific leg of a super order.

Users can cancel a pending/active super order using the order ID. There is no body for request and response for this call. On successful completion of request ‘202 Accepted’ response status code will appear.

**Before you start with Cancel Super Order Leg API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Whitelist your [static IP](/api/v2/authentication/set-ip). Orders only go through from it.
3. Copy the `orderId` from the [super order book](/api/v2/super-orders/get-super-orders) and pick the leg to cancel under Path Parameters below.

> **NOTICE:** Order Placement, Modification and Cancellation APIs requires Static IP whitelisting - [refer here](/api/v2/authentication#setup-static-ip)

## Sample request for Cancel Super Order Leg API

Cancel the target leg of a Super Order. There is no request body. Replace the order ID (`112111182198` here) and access token with your own, and pick the leg to cancel.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request DELETE \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/super/orders/112111182198/TARGET_LEG \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for Cancel Super Order Leg API

Values are illustrative.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "orderId": "112111182198",
  "orderStatus": "CANCELLED"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Path Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| order-id | string | Yes | Order ID of the Order being cancelled |
| order-leg | string | Yes | Order Leg to be cancelled Values: `ENTRY_LEG`, `TARGET_LEG`, `STOP_LOSS_LEG`. |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| order-id | string | No | Order specific identification generated by Dhan |
| orderStatus | enum string | No | Last updated order status [see annexure](/api/v2/annexure#order-status) Values: `TRANSIT`, `PENDING`, `REJECTED`, `CANCELLED`, `PART_TRADED`, `TRADED`, `EXPIRED`, `MODIFIED`, `TRIGGERED`. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Leg cancelled |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X DELETE https://api.dhan.co/v2/super/orders/{order-id}/{order-leg} \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Conditional and Multi Order

Conditional and Multi Order: price and technical indicator conditional orders, and multi orders with up to 15 orders in one Dhan API call.

The Conditional and Multi Order API is a special set of APIs which lets you place order on the basis of set conditions. These conditions can be based on price or technical indicators or a combination of both. You can set one or multiple orders to be triggered when the condition is met.

When the conditional order is triggered, you will receive a postback update if set up [here](/api/v2/postback).

| Method | Endpoint | Description |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| POST | [`/alerts/orders`](/api/v2/conditional-triggers/place-conditional-trigger) | Place Conditional Order |
| POST | [`/alerts/multi/orders`](/api/v2/conditional-triggers/place-multi-order) | Place Multi Order |
| PUT | [`/alerts/orders/{alertId}`](/api/v2/conditional-triggers/modify-conditional-trigger) | Modify Conditional Order |
| DELETE | [`/alerts/orders/{alertId}`](/api/v2/conditional-triggers/delete-conditional-trigger) | Delete Conditional Order |
| GET | [`/alerts/orders/{alertId}`](/api/v2/conditional-triggers/get-conditional-trigger-by-id) | Get Conditional Order by ID |
| GET | [`/alerts/orders`](/api/v2/conditional-triggers/get-all-conditional-triggers) | Get All Conditional Orders |

> **note:** - Conditional Orders are currently supported only for Equities and Indices.
> - You can receive a postback update by providing a Webhook URL ([here](/api/v2/postback)) while generating the Access Token.

## Configure P&L Based Exit

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/pnlExit

Configure automatic exit rules based on cumulative profit or loss thresholds.

> **Note:** The configured P&L based exit remains active for the current day and is reset at the end of the trading session.

**Before you start with Configure P&L Based Exit API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Whitelist your [static IP](/api/v2/authentication/set-ip). Orders only go through from it.
3. Set `profitValue` and `lossValue` in rupees and pick the product types to cover.

> **NOTICE:** Order Placement, Modification and Cancellation APIs requires Static IP whitelisting - [refer here](/api/v2/authentication#setup-static-ip)

## Sample request for Configure P&L Based Exit API

Exit intraday positions at ₹1,500 profit or ₹500 loss, without the kill switch. Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/pnlExit \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "dhanClientId": "<your-client-id>",
    "profitValue": 1500,
    "lossValue": 500,
    "productType": [
      "INTRADAY"
    ],
    "enableKillSwitch": false
  }'
```


| Parameter | Data Type | Description |
|-----------|-----------|-------------|
| pnlExitStatus | string | P&L based exit status: `ACTIVE` `INACTIVE` |
| message | string | Status of Conditional Trigger |

## Sample response for Configure P&L Based Exit API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "pnlExitStatus": "ACTIVE",
  "message": "P&L based exit configured successfully"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | No | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| profitValue | float | No | Maximum portfolio-level profit to trigger exit |
| lossValue | float | No | Maximum portfolio-level loss to trigger exit |
| productType[0] | enum | No | Product types applicable (array): INTRADAY or DELIVERY Values: `INTRADAY`, `DELIVERY`. |
| enableKillSwitch | boolean | No | Indicates if kill switch is enabled (true or false) |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | P&L exit configured |
| 401 | Unauthorized |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/pnlExit \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Convert Position

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions/convert

Convert position between product types (e.g., INTRADAY to CNC).

Users can convert their open position from intraday to delivery or delivery to intraday.

**Before you start with Convert Position API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Whitelist your [static IP](/api/v2/authentication/set-ip). Orders only go through from it.
3. Pick an open position from [Get Positions](/api/v2/portfolio/get-positions) and enter its `securityId` and quantity.

> **NOTICE:** Order Placement, Modification and Cancellation APIs requires Static IP whitelisting - [refer here](/api/v2/authentication#setup-static-ip)

## Sample request for Convert Position API

Convert 1 share of an intraday Vodafone Idea (`securityId` 14366) long position to delivery. Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions/convert \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "dhanClientId": "<your-client-id>",
    "fromProductType": "INTRADAY",
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "positionType": "LONG",
    "securityId": "14366",
    "convertQty": 1,
    "toProductType": "CNC"
  }'
```


No response body. On successful completion of request, `202 Accepted` response status code will appear.

## Sample response for Convert Position API

`202 Accepted`. There is no response body.

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | No | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| fromProductType | enum | No | From Product type Values: `CNC`, `INTRADAY`, `MARGIN`. |
| exchangeSegment | enum | No | Exchange Segment (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_EQ`, `NSE_FNO`, `BSE_EQ`, `BSE_FNO`, `MCX_COMM`. |
| positionType | enum | No | Position to convert Values: `LONG`, `SHORT`, `CLOSED`. |
| securityId | string | No | Exchange standard ID for each scrip. refer here |
| convertQty | int | No | Quantity to convert |
| toProductType | enum | No | To Product type Values: `CNC`, `INTRADAY`, `MARGIN`. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 202 | Conversion accepted |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions/convert \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Data APIs

All endpoints for market data, historical charts, option chains, and account statements.

## Market Quote

| Method | Endpoint | Description |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| POST | [Get LTP](/api/v2/market-quote/get-ltp) | Get last traded price for instruments |

## Delete Conditional Order

`DELETE` https://api.dhan.co/v2/alerts/orders/{alertId}

Delete an existing conditional order.

Cancel a conditional order before its condition is met, using its `alertId`.

**Before you start with Delete Conditional Order API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Whitelist your [static IP](/api/v2/authentication/set-ip). Orders only go through from it.
3. Copy the `alertId` from [Get All Conditional Orders](/api/v2/conditional-triggers/get-all-conditional-triggers) and enter it under Path Parameters below.

> **NOTICE:** Order Placement, Modification and Cancellation APIs requires Static IP whitelisting - [refer here](/api/v2/authentication#setup-static-ip)

## Sample request for Delete Conditional Order API

Delete a conditional order. There is no request body. Replace the alert ID (`1100000123` here) and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request DELETE \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/alerts/orders/1100000123 \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for Delete Conditional Order API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "alertId": "1100000123",
  "alertStatus": "CANCELLED"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Path Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| alertId | string | Yes | Alert specific identification generated by Dhan |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| alertId | string | No | Alert specific identification generated by Dhan |
| alertStatus | enum string | No | Last updated alert status [see annexure](/api/v2/annexure#status) Values: `ACTIVE`, `TRIGGERED`, `EXPIRED`, `CANCELLED`. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Conditional trigger deleted |
| 401 | Unauthorized |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X DELETE https://api.dhan.co/v2/alerts/orders/{alertId} \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## DhanHQ API

Introduction to DhanHQ Trading & Investment API

# Getting Started

DhanHQ API is a state-of-the-art platform that enables you to build trading and investment services, applications, and strategies.

It provides a set of REST-like APIs that integrate directly with our trading platform. With DhanHQ APIs, you can execute and modify orders in real time, manage portfolios, access live market data, and more through a fast and reliable API collection.

Start Trading & Investing with Superfast APIs

The platform uses resource-based URLs and supports JSON as well as form-encoded requests. Responses are returned in JSON format using standard HTTP response codes, verbs, and authentication methods.

[DhanHQ Python Client pip install dhanhq View on GitHub](https://github.com/dhan-oss/DhanHQ-py)

## Built for algo traders

Whether you run your own strategies, backtest ideas, automate your setup or need reliable market data and fast trading APIs, DhanHQ APIs cover the full workflow.

Trade your strategies

Backtest your ideas

Automate your setup

Get reliable market data

Businesses built on DhanHQ

Fintechs
Banks
Wealth Platforms
Sub Brokers
AI & Agents
Trading Tools
Algo Providers
& more

## Structure

#### REST

All **GET** and **DELETE** request parameters go as query parameters, and **POST** and **PUT** parameters as form-encoded. User has to input an access token in the header for every request.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/ \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{Request JSON}'
```

#### Python

Install Python Package directly using following command in command line.

```bash title="Terminal"
pip install dhanhq
```
This installs entire DhanHQ Python Client along with the required packages. Now, you can start using DhanHQ Client with your Python script.

You can now import 'dhanhq' module and connect to your Dhan account.

```python title="Python"
from dhanhq import dhanhq

dhan = dhanhq("client_id","access_token")
```

## Errors

Error responses come with the error code and message generated internally by the system. The sample structure of error response is shown below.

```json title="Error response"
{
    "errorType": "",
    "errorCode": "",
    "errorMessage": ""
}
```

You can find detailed error code and message under [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure).

## Rate Limits

| API Category | Rate | Per-request cap | Daily limit |
|--------------|------|-----------------|-------------|
| **Order APIs** | 10 req/sec | — | 7,000 |
| **Data APIs** | 5 req/sec | — | 100,000 |
| **Market Quote** | 1 req/sec | 1,000 instruments | — |
| **Option Chain** | 1 req / 3 sec
10 unique scrips / 8 sec | 1 scrip | — |
| **Non Trading APIs** | 20 req/sec
Unlimited per minute and per hour | — | Unlimited |

## EDIS Status Inquiry

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/inquire/{isin}

Check eDIS approval status for a stock by ISIN. Pass ALL to check all holdings.

You can check the status of stock whether it is approved and marked for sell action. Users have to enter the ISIN of the stock. An International Securities Identification Number (ISIN) is a 12-digit alphanumeric code that uniquely identifies a specific security. You can get ISIN of portfolio stocks, in response of holdings API.

Alternatively, you can pass "ALL" instead of ISIN to get eDIS status of all holdings in your portfolio.

**Before you start with EDIS Status Inquiry API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Enter the stock's ISIN under Path Parameters below.

## Sample request for EDIS Status Inquiry API

Check the eDIS status of Vodafone Idea (ISIN `INE669E01016`). Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/inquire/INE669E01016 \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for EDIS Status Inquiry API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "clientId": "1000000132",
  "isin": "INE669E01016",
  "totalQty": "1",
  "aprvdQty": "1",
  "status": "SUCCESS",
  "remark": "eDIS transaction done successfully"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Path Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| isin | string | Yes | ISIN of portfolio stocks, derived from holdings API |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| clientId | string | No | User specific identification |
| isin | string | No | International Securities Identification Number |
| totalQty | string | No | Total number of shares for given stock |
| aprvdQty | string | No | Number of approved stocks |
| status | enum string | No | Status of the eDIS order Values: `SUCCESS`, `FAILURE`. |
| remark | string | No | Remarks of the order status |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | eDIS status |
| 401 | Unauthorized |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/inquire/{isin} \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

---

## EDIS

EDIS: CDSL T-PIN and eDIS authorisation to sell stocks from your demat account with the Dhan API.

To sell holding stocks, one needs to complete the CDSL eDIS flow, generate T-PIN & mark stock to complete the sell action.

| Method | Endpoint | Description |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| GET | [`/edis/tpin`](/api/v2/edis/generate-tpin) | Generate T-PIN |
| POST | [`/edis/from`](/api/v2/edis/generate-edis-form) | Retrieve escaped html form & enter T-PIN |
| GET | [`/edis/inquire/{isin}`](/api/v2/edis/edis-inquiry) | Inquire the status of stock for edis approval. |

## Exit All Positions

`DELETE` https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions

Exit all open positions at market price.

Exit all active positions and cancel all open orders for the current trading day.

**Before you start with Exit All Positions API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Whitelist your [static IP](/api/v2/authentication/set-ip). Orders only go through from it.
3. This exits **every** open position and cancels all open orders right away.

> **NOTICE:** Order Placement, Modification and Cancellation APIs requires Static IP whitelisting - [refer here](/api/v2/authentication#setup-static-ip)

## Sample request for Exit All Positions API

Exit all positions and cancel all open orders. There is no request body. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request DELETE \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


No response body. On successful completion of request, `202 Accepted` response status code will appear.

## Sample response for Exit All Positions API

`202 Accepted`. There is no response body.

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | All positions exited |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X DELETE https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

---

## Expired Options Data

Expired Options Data: minute-level OHLC, IV and OI for expired index and stock options.

This API gives you expired options contract data. We have pre processed data for you to get it on rolling basis i.e. you can fetch last 5 years of strike wise data based on ATM and upto 10 strikes above and below. In addition to that, the data values are open, high, low, close, implied volatility, volume, open interest and spot information as well.

| Method | Endpoint | Description |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| POST | [`/charts/rollingoption`](/api/v2/expired-options-data/get-expired-options-data) | Get Continuous Expired Options Contract data |

## Company Info

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/companyinfo

Fetch selected fundamental metrics for a single equity instrument. Pass the required metric groups and receive the latest available snapshot across company classification, industry benchmarks, shareholding, valuation, profitability, price performance, and book value.

Company information for a stock is available in three sections: the company overview, which includes market cap, sector, EPS, EBITDA, book value and the 52-week range; the return ratios, such as P/E, P/B, ROE and debt to equity; and the shareholding pattern.

**Before you start with Company Info API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Pick the stock and the metric sections you need. Only those are returned.

## Sample request for Company Info API

Company overview (`CO`) for Vodafone Idea (`14366`). Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/companyinfo \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "securityId": "14366",
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "instrument": "EQUITY",
    "metrics": [
      "CO"
    ]
  }'
```


## Sample response for Company Info API

Values are illustrative.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "securityId": "14366",
  "data": {
    "CO": {
      "MARKET_CAP": "140520.6",
      "INDUSTRY": "Telecom - Cellular & Fixed line services",
      "SECTOR": "Telecommunication",
      "DIVIDEND_YIELD": "0",
      "EPS": "-2.61",
      "EBITDA": "18240",
      "BOOK_VALUE": "-6.48",
      "FIFTY_TWO_WEEK_HIGH": "15.79",
      "FIFTY_TWO_WEEK_LOW": "8.13"
    }
  }
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#data-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| securityId | string | Yes | Exchange standard ID for each security. For eg. "1333" |
| exchangeSegment | enum | Yes | Exchange segment of the instrument NSE_EQ and BSE_EQ (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_EQ`, `BSE_EQ`. |
| instrument | enum | Yes | Instrument Type EQUITY (see Annexure) Values: `EQUITY`. |
| metrics | array | Yes | Metric sections to fetch. Only those passed here are returned. For eg. [ "CO" ] (see Annexure) Values: `CO`, `RATIOS`, `SHP`. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Successful operation |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/companyinfo \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Market Movers

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/marketmovers

Fetch top options, futures, and stocks ranked by open interest, traded volume, or price movement. Select the instrument type(s) and ranking category to retrieve the highest-ranked instruments.

Returns the top gainers and losers, the most traded instruments, and the instruments with the highest open interest or the biggest open interest gains and losses, across stocks, futures and options.

**Before you start with Market Movers API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Pick the segment, instrument type and ranking category. Futures and options also need an expiry.

## Sample request for Market Movers API

Top 5 price gainers in the NIFTY 50. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/marketmovers \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "instrument": [
      "EQUITY"
    ],
    "category": "PRICE_GAINERS",
    "universe": "NIFTY_50",
    "limit": 5
  }'
```


## Sample response for Market Movers API

Values are illustrative. `data` has one entry per instrument, ranked; one is shown here.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
  "category": "PRICE_GAINERS",
  "data": [
    {
      "securityId": "2885",
      "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
      "tradingSymbol": "RELIANCE",
      "displayName": "Reliance Industries",
      "instrument": "EQUITY",
      "expiry": null,
      "strikePrice": 0.0,
      "tickSize": 0.1,
      "lotSize": 1,
      "ltp": 1452.6,
      "change": 38.4,
      "changePercent": 2.72,
      "volume": 12456780,
      "tradedValue": 18094580000.0,
      "underlyingSecurityId": "",
      "underlyingLtp": 0.0,
      "premiumDiscount": 0.0,
      "premiumDiscountPercent": 0.0,
      "openInterest": 0,
      "openInterestChange": 0,
      "openInterestChangePercent": 0.0,
      "putCallRatio": 0.0
    }
  ]
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#data-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| exchangeSegment | enum | Yes | Exchange segment of the instruments to rank (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_FNO`, `BSE_FNO`, `NSE_COMM`, `MCX_COMM`, `NSE_EQ`, `BSE_EQ`. |
| instrument | array | Yes | Instrument types for which data is to be fetched (see Annexure) Values: `OPTIDX`, `OPTSTK`, `OPTFUT`, `FUTIDX`, `FUTSTK`, `FUTCOM`, `EQUITY`. |
| category | enum | Yes | Ranking category to apply. Supported categories depend on the selected instrument (see Annexure) Values: `HIGHEST_OI`, `OI_GAINERS`, `OI_LOSERS`, `TOP_VOLUME`, `PRICE_GAINERS`, `PRICE_LOSERS`. |
| expiry | string | No | Expiry date of the derivative contracts in YYYY-MM-DD format. Required when instrument is an option or future contract. Optional and ignored when instrument is EQUITY. For eg. "2026-07-28" |
| universe | enum | No | Stock universe within which instruments are ranked, Applicable only when instrument is EQUITY Values: `ALL`, `FNO_STOCKS`, `NIFTY_50`, `NIFTY_BANK`, `FINNIFTY`, `INDIA_VIX`, `NIFTY_MIDCAP`, `NIFTY_NEXT_50`, `NIFTY_SMALLCAP_50`, `NIFTY_MIDCAP_50`, `NIFTY_100`, `NIFTY_200`, `NIFTY_500`, `NIFTY_MIDCAP_100`, `NIFTY_MIDCAP_150`, `NIFTY_SMALLCAP_100`, `NIFTY_SMALLCAP_250`, `NIFTY_MICROCAP_250`, `NIFTY_AUTO`, `NIFTY_PRIVATE_BANK`, `NIFTY_FMCG`, `NIFTY_ENERGY`, `NIFTY_INFRA`, `NIFTY_IT`, `NIFTY_MEDIA`, `NIFTY_METAL`, `NIFTY_MNC`, `NIFTY_PHARMA`, `NIFTY_PSU_BANK`, `NIFTY_REALTY`, `NIFTY_SERVICES_SECTOR`, `NIFTY_CONSUMPTION`, `GIFT_NIFTY`. |
| limit | int | No | Maximum number of instruments to return. Maximum allowed value is 100. For eg. "20" Constraints: 1-100. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Successful operation |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/marketmovers \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Live News Headline

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/newsheadline

Fetch the latest news headlines as of now, filterable by category and stock, capped at limit (max 50) items.

Returns the latest market and company news headlines, each with a sentiment. The news can be filtered by category, such as companies, IPO or the economy, and by stock.

**Before you start with Live News Headline API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Pick news categories and how many items you want (up to 50).

## Sample request for Live News Headline API

The latest 10 headlines across all categories. Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/newsheadline \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "dhanClientId": "<your-client-id>",
    "categories": [
      "ALL"
    ],
    "limit": 10
  }'
```


## Sample response for Live News Headline API

Values are illustrative. `latestNews` has one entry per headline; one is shown here with placeholder text.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "data": {
    "latestNews": [
      {
        "newsObject": {
          "overallSentiment": "NEUTRAL",
          "title": "<headline>",
          "text": "<summary>"
        },
        "category": "COMPANIES",
        "subCategory": "<sub-category>",
        "publishDate": "2026-11-13 10:42:00",
        "stockName": "Vodafone Idea",
        "isinCode": "INE669E01016",
        "smSymbol": "IDEA",
        "nseScripCode": 14366,
        "bseScripCode": 532822,
        "displaySymbol": "IDEA",
        "tickSize": 0.01,
        "lotSize": 1
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#data-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | Yes | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| categories | array | Yes | News category filter, (see Annexure) for valid enums. Values: `ALL`, `COMPANIES`, `EQUITY_MARKETS`, `DEBT_MARKETS`, `IPO`, `GLOBAL`, `INDIAN_ECONOMY`, `GLOBAL_ECONOMY`, `CURRENCY`, `COMMODITIES`, `CRYPTOCURRENCIES`, `GOVERNMENT_POLICY_AND_REGULATION`, `INFRASTRUCTURE`, `INTERNATIONAL_TRADE`, `INVESTMENT_IDEAS`, `REAL_ESTATE`, `STARTUPS`, `TECHNOLOGY`, `MUTUAL_FUNDS`. |
| limit | int | Yes | Max number of news items to return, counted from the current date/time backwards. Allowed range is 1 to 50. For eg. 50 |
| universe | enum | No | Scopes the news to the caller's portfolio or watchlist. Optional - omit for no scoping (must be a string, not null, not empty). Values: `PORTFOLIO`, `WATCHLIST`. |
| stockList | array | No | Security IDs to filter news by specific stocks. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Successful operation |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/newsheadline \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

---

## Technical Data

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/technical

Fetch technical indicator values for an instrument. Pass the indicators you need - only those are computed and returned. All values are point-in-time as of the latest closed candle for the requested timeframe.

Technical indicators such as SMA, EMA, RSI, MACD, ADX, ATR, Stochastic and pivot points can be fetched for a stock or an index. They are computed on 1, 5 or 15 minute candles or on daily candles, depending on the timeframe you choose.

**Before you start with Technical Data API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Pick the instrument, candle size and the indicators you need. Only those are returned.

## Sample request for Technical Data API

20-day SMA and 14-day RSI for Vodafone Idea (`14366`) on daily candles. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/technical \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "securityId": "14366",
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "instrument": "EQUITY",
    "timeframe": "D",
    "indicators": [
      "SMA_20",
      "RSI_14"
    ]
  }'
```


## Sample response for Technical Data API

Values are illustrative. `data` has one entry per requested indicator; the fields inside each entry are not documented yet, so `value` here is a placeholder.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "securityId": "14366",
  "timeframe": "D",
  "data": {
    "SMA_20": {
      "value": 12.84
    },
    "RSI_14": {
      "value": 58.6
    }
  }
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| securityId | string | Yes | Exchange standard ID for each security. For eg. "1333" |
| exchangeSegment | enum | Yes | Exchange segment of the instrument (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_EQ`, `IDX_I`. |
| instrument | enum | Yes | Valid Instrument types INDEX and EQUITY (see Annexure) Values: `INDEX`, `EQUITY`. |
| timeframe | enum | Yes | Candle size used to compute all indicators in this request (see Annexure) Values: `1`, `5`, `15`, `D`. |
| indicators | array | Yes | Indicator identifiers to fetch. Only those passed here are computed and returned. For eg. [ "SMA_20", "RSI_14", "MACD_HIST" ] (see Annexure) Values: `SMA_5`, `SMA_10`, `SMA_20`, `SMA_50`, `SMA_100`, `SMA_200`, `EMA_5`, `EMA_10`, `EMA_20`, `EMA_50`, `EMA_100`, `EMA_200`, `RSI_14`, `MACD_HIST`, `STOCH`, `STOCHRSI_14`, `ATR_14`, `ADX_14`, `UO`, `ROC`, `WILLR`, `PIVOT_CLASSIC`, `PIVOT_FIBONACCI`, `PIVOT_CAMARILLA`. |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Successful operation |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/data/technical \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Funds

Funds: available balance, fund limits and margin calculator (single order and multiple orders) with the Dhan API.

Users can get details about the fund requirements or available funds (with margin requirements) in their Trading Account.

| Method | Endpoint | Description |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| POST | [`/margincalculator`](/api/v2/funds/calculate-margin) | Margin requirement for any order |
| GET | [`/fundlimit`](/api/v2/funds/get-fund-limits) | Retrieve Available Fund Limits |
| POST | [`/margincalculator/multi`](/api/v2/funds/calculate-multi-margin) | Calculate Margin for Multiple Orders |

## Generate eDIS Form

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/bulkform

Generate an eDIS bulk authorisation form for one or more ISINs.

Retrieve escaped html form of CDSL and enter T-PIN to mark the stock for EDIS approval. User has to render this form at their end to unescape. You can get ISIN of portfolio stocks, in response of holdings API.

**Before you start with Generate eDIS Form API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Generate a T-PIN first with [Generate T-PIN](/api/v2/edis/generate-tpin).
3. Enter the ISIN of each stock to authorise. ISINs are in the [instrument list](/api/v2/instruments).

## Sample request for Generate eDIS Form API

Generate the eDIS form for Vodafone Idea (ISIN `INE669E01016`) on NSE. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/bulkform \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "isin": [
      "INE669E01016"
    ],
    "exchange": "NSE",
    "segment": "EQ",
    "bulk": false
  }'
```


## Sample response for Generate eDIS Form API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "dhanClientId": "1000000132",
  "edisFormHtml": "<!DOCTYPE html> <html>...</html>"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| isin | array | Yes | List of ISINs to approve for selling (from holdings API) |
| exchange | enum | Yes | Exchange Values: `NSE`, `BSE`, `MCX`, `ALL`. |
| segment | enum | Yes | Trading segment Values: `EQ`, `COMM`, `FNO`. |
| bulk | boolean | No | Whether to generate form for bulk EDIS |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | No | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| edisFormHtml | string | No | Escaped HTML form to be rendered on the client side |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | eDIS form HTML returned |
| 401 | Unauthorized |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/bulkform \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Generate T-PIN

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/tpin

Request a T-PIN on your registered mobile number for eDIS authorization.

Get T-Pin on your registered mobile number using this API.

**Before you start with Generate T-PIN API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. This sends a T-PIN by SMS to your registered mobile number.

## Sample request for Generate T-PIN API

Request a T-PIN. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/tpin \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```

## Response

`202 Accepted` — the T-PIN is sent to your registered mobile number. This endpoint returns no response body.

## Sample response for Generate T-PIN API

`202 Accepted`. There is no response body; the T-PIN is sent by SMS to your registered mobile number.

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 202 | Accepted — T-PIN sent to your registered mobile number. No response body. |
| 401 | Unauthorized |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/edis/tpin \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Get All Conditional Orders

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/alerts/orders

Retrieve all conditional orders for the authenticated account.

## Get Daily Historical Data

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/historical

Get daily OHLC candle data for backtesting and analysis.

Retrieve OHLC & Volume of daily candle for desired instrument. The data for any scrip is available back upto the date of its inception.

You provide the security ID, exchange segment and instrument type of the scrip, along with a start date and an end date. The end date is non-inclusive. For F&O instruments, Open Interest data can also be fetched, and the expiry code can be passed for derivatives.

**Before you start with Get Daily Historical Data API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Pick the instrument from the [instrument list](/api/v2/instruments) and a date range.

## Sample request for Get Daily Historical Data API

Daily candles for Vodafone Idea (`14366`) from 10 to 12 November 2026 (`toDate` is not included). Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/historical \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "securityId": "14366",
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "instrument": "EQUITY",
    "expiryCode": 0,
    "oi": "false",
    "fromDate": "2026-11-10",
    "toDate": "2026-11-13"
  }'
```


## Sample response for Get Daily Historical Data API

Values are illustrative. Each field is a list with one entry per candle, in the same order. `timestamp` is epoch seconds.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "open": [
    12.7,
    12.81,
    12.88
  ],
  "high": [
    12.92,
    12.95,
    13.05
  ],
  "low": [
    12.62,
    12.74,
    12.84
  ],
  "close": [
    12.81,
    12.88,
    12.97
  ],
  "volume": [
    288140000,
    301220000,
    312450000
  ],
  "timestamp": [
    1794249000,
    1794335400,
    1794421800
  ],
  "open_interest": [
    0,
    0,
    0
  ]
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#data-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| securityId | string | No | Exchange standard ID for each scrip. refer here |
| exchangeSegment | enum | No | Exchange & segment (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_EQ`, `NSE_FNO`, `BSE_EQ`, `BSE_FNO`, `MCX_COMM`, `IDX_I`. |
| instrument | string | No | Instrument type of the scrip (see Annexure) |
| expiryCode | int | No | Expiry of the instrument (for derivatives) (see Annexure) |
| oi | string | No | Open Interest data for F&O: true or false |
| fromDate | string | No | Start date (YYYY-MM-DD) |
| toDate | string | No | End date (YYYY-MM-DD, non-inclusive) |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| open | float | No | Open price of the timeframe |
| high | float | No | High price in the timeframe |
| low | float | No | Low price in the timeframe |
| close | float | No | Close price of the timeframe |
| volume | int | No | Volume traded in the timeframe |
| open_interest | int | No | Open interest (for F&O instruments) |
| timestamp | int | No | Epoch timestamp |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Historical OHLC data |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/historical \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

---

## Historical Rolling Options Data

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/rollingoption

Fetch expired options contract data on a rolling basis with OHLC, IV, OI and spot information. Up to one day per call. Last 5 years available.

Fetch expired options data on a rolling basis, along with the Open Interest, Implied Volatility, OHLC, Volume as well as information about the spot. You can fetch up to one day of data in a single API call. Expired options data is stored on a minute level, based on strike price relative to spot (example ATM, ATM+10, ATM-10, etc.).

You can fetch data upto last 5 years. We have added both Index Options and Stock Options data on this.

**Before you start with Historical Rolling Options Data API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Choose the underlying, expiry (week or month), strike (for example `ATM` or `ATM+1`) and option type.

## Sample request for Historical Rolling Options Data API

1-minute data for the near-week NIFTY (`13`) ATM call on 27 October 2026. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/rollingoption \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_FNO",
    "interval": "1",
    "securityId": "13",
    "instrument": "OPTIDX",
    "expiryFlag": "WEEK",
    "expiryCode": "1",
    "strike": "ATM",
    "drvOptionType": "CALL",
    "requiredData": [
      "open",
      "high",
      "low",
      "close",
      "iv",
      "volume",
      "oi",
      "spot",
      "strike"
    ],
    "fromDate": "2026-10-27",
    "toDate": "2026-10-28"
  }'
```


## Sample response for Historical Rolling Options Data API

Values are illustrative. `pe` is `null` because a call was requested. Each field is a list with one entry per candle.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "data": {
    "ce": {
      "open": [
        142.5,
        140.1
      ],
      "high": [
        145.0,
        141.8
      ],
      "low": [
        139.6,
        138.9
      ],
      "close": [
        140.2,
        139.4
      ],
      "volume": [
        1854000,
        1126500
      ],
      "iv": [
        13.8,
        13.7
      ],
      "oi": [
        6234500,
        6301250
      ],
      "spot": [
        25812.4,
        25806.9
      ],
      "strike": [
        25800.0,
        25800.0
      ],
      "timestamp": [
        1793072700,
        1793072760
      ]
    },
    "pe": null
  }
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#data-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| exchangeSegment | enum | No | Exchange & Segment (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_FNO`, `BSE_FNO`. |
| interval | enum | No | Minute intervals Values: `1`, `5`, `15`, `25`, `60`. |
| securityId | string | No | Underlying security ID |
| instrument | enum | No | Instrument type (see Annexure) Values: `OPTIDX`, `OPTSTK`. |
| expiryFlag | enum | No | Expiry Flag Values: `WEEK`, `MONTH`. |
| expiryCode | enum | No | 1=Near, 2=Next, 3=Far Values: `1`, `2`, `3`. |
| strike | string | No | ATM for At the Money<br/>Upto ATM+10 / ATM-10 for Index Options near expiry<br/>Upto ATM+3 / ATM-3 for all other contracts |
| drvOptionType | enum | No | Option Type Values: `CALL`, `PUT`. |
| requiredData | array | No | Array of data fields: open, high, low, close, iv, volume, strike, oi, spot Values: `open`, `high`, `low`, `close`, `iv`, `volume`, `strike`, `oi`, `spot`. |
| fromDate | string | No | Start Date (YYYY-MM-DD) |
| toDate | string | No | End Date (YYYY-MM-DD) |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| data.ce.open | array | No | Array of open prices for CE |
| data.ce.high | array | No | Array of high prices for CE |
| data.ce.low | array | No | Array of low prices for CE |
| data.ce.close | array | No | Array of close prices for CE |
| data.ce.volume | array | No | Array of volumes for CE |
| data.ce.iv | array | No | Array of implied volatilities for CE |
| data.ce.oi | array | No | Array of open interest for CE |
| data.ce.spot | array | No | Array of underlying spot prices for CE |
| data.ce.strike | array | No | Array of strike prices for CE |
| data.ce.timestamp | array | No | Array of epoch timestamps for CE |
| data.pe | object | No | Object containing PE data points analogous to CE |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Expired options data |
| 401 | Unauthorized |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/rollingoption \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Get Fund Limits

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/fundlimit

Retrieve available balance and fund limits.

Get all information of your trading account like balance, margin utilised, collateral, etc.

Your **available balance** and margins: start-of-day limit, margin used, collateral from pledged shares and **withdrawable balance**.

**Before you start with Get Fund Limits API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.

## Sample request for Get Fund Limits API

Fetch your fund limits. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/fundlimit \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for Get Fund Limits API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "dhanClientId": "1000000132",
  "availabelBalance": 25000.0,
  "sodLimit": 25000.0,
  "collateralAmount": 0.0,
  "receiveableAmount": 0.0,
  "utilizedAmount": 12.97,
  "blockedPayoutAmount": 0.0,
  "withdrawableBalance": 24987.03
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | No | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| availabelBalance | float | No | Primary field to check the overall usable balance |
| sodLimit | float | No | Start of day limit |
| collateralAmount | float | No | Amount received against pledged shares |
| receiveableAmount | float | No | Any newly added funds or updated profit |
| utilizedAmount | float | No | Funds used for executing any trades |
| blockedPayoutAmount | float | No | Funds blocked for withdrawal |
| withdrawableBalance | float | No | Amount available to withdraw |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Fund limit details |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/fundlimit \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Get Holdings

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/holdings

Retrieve all holdings in demat account.

Users can retrieve all holdings bought/sold in previous trading sessions. All T1 and delivered quantities can be fetched.

**Before you start with Get Holdings API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.

## Sample request for Get Holdings API

Fetch your holdings. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/holdings \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for Get Holdings API

```json title="200 OK"
[
  {
    "exchange": "ALL",
    "tradingSymbol": "IDEA",
    "securityId": "14366",
    "isin": "INE669E01016",
    "totalQty": 1,
    "dpQty": 1,
    "t1Qty": 0,
    "mtf_t1_qty": 0,
    "mtf_qty": 0,
    "availableQty": 1,
    "collateralQty": 0,
    "avgCostPrice": 12.97,
    "lastTradedPrice": 12.97
  }
]
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| exchange | enum string | No | Exchange Values: `NSE`, `BSE`, `MCX`, `ALL`. |
| tradingSymbol | string | No | Refer Trading Symbol in Tables |
| securityId | string | No | Exchange standard ID for each scrip. refer here |
| isin | string | No | ISIN of the stock |
| totalQty | int | No | T1 qty + Delivered qty |
| dpQty | int | No | Delivered Quantity |
| t1Qty | int | No | T1 Qty |
| mtf_t1_qty | int | No | MTF T1 quantities |
| mtf_qty | int | No | MTF delivered quantities |
| availableQty | int | No | Total quantity - Pledged quantity |
| collateralQty | int | No | Pledged quantity |
| avgCostPrice | float | No | Average buying price of the stock |
| lastTradedPrice | float | No | Last traded price (LTP) for the scrip |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | List of holdings |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/holdings \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Get Intraday Historical Data

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/intraday

Get intraday OHLC candle data (1, 5, 15, 25, 60 minute intervals).

Retrieve Open, High, Low, Close, OI & Volume of 1, 5, 15, 25 and 60 min candle for desired instrument for last 5 years. This data available for all exchanges and segments for all active instruments.

> **Note:** The data size is very large in this scenario and only 90 days of data can be polled at once for any of the above time intervals. It is recommended that you store this data at your end for day-to-day analysis.

**Before you start with Get Intraday Historical Data API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Pick the instrument from the [instrument list](/api/v2/instruments), the candle interval and a date range.

## Sample request for Get Intraday Historical Data API

5-minute candles for Vodafone Idea (`14366`) on 13 November 2026. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/intraday \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "securityId": "14366",
    "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
    "instrument": "EQUITY",
    "interval": "5",
    "oi": "false",
    "fromDate": "2026-11-13",
    "toDate": "2026-11-14"
  }'
```


## Sample response for Get Intraday Historical Data API

Values are illustrative. Each field is a list with one entry per candle, in the same order. `timestamp` is epoch seconds.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "open": [
    12.88,
    12.93,
    12.95
  ],
  "high": [
    12.95,
    12.97,
    12.99
  ],
  "low": [
    12.86,
    12.91,
    12.93
  ],
  "close": [
    12.93,
    12.95,
    12.96
  ],
  "volume": [
    18450000,
    9120000,
    7380000
  ],
  "timestamp": [
    1794541500,
    1794541800,
    1794542100
  ],
  "open_interest": [
    0,
    0,
    0
  ]
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#data-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| securityId | string | No | Exchange standard ID for each scrip. refer here |
| exchangeSegment | enum | No | Exchange & segment (see Annexure) Values: `NSE_EQ`, `NSE_FNO`, `BSE_EQ`, `BSE_FNO`, `MCX_COMM`, `IDX_I`. |
| instrument | string | No | Instrument type of the scrip (see Annexure) |
| interval | enum | No | Minute intervals Values: `1`, `5`, `15`, `25`, `60`. |
| oi | string | No | Open Interest data for F&O: true or false |
| fromDate | string | No | Start date (YYYY-MM-DD) |
| toDate | string | No | End date (YYYY-MM-DD) |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| open | float | No | Open price of the timeframe |
| high | float | No | High price in the timeframe |
| low | float | No | Low price in the timeframe |
| close | float | No | Close price of the timeframe |
| volume | int | No | Volume traded in the timeframe |
| open_interest | int | No | Open interest (for F&O instruments) |
| timestamp | int | No | Epoch timestamp |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Intraday OHLC data |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X POST https://api.dhan.co/v2/charts/intraday \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json"
```

## Kill Switch Status

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/killswitch

Check whether kill switch is active for the current trading day.

The API allows you to check kill switch status for your account - whether it is active for the current trade or not.

**Before you start with Kill Switch Status API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.

## Sample request for Kill Switch Status API

Fetch the kill switch status. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/killswitch \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```

```json title="200 OK"
  "dhanClientId": "1000000132",
  "killSwitchStatus": "ACTIVE"
}
```

### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | No | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| killSwitchStatus | string | No | Status of Kill Switch Values: `ACTIVE`, `INACTIVE`. |

Ledger for 1 to 13 November 2026. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url 'https://api.dhan.co/v2/ledger?from-date=2026-11-01&to-date=2026-11-13' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```

```json title="200 OK"
    "voucherdate": "Nov 03, 2026",
    "exchange": "ALL",
    "voucherdesc": "PAYIN",
    "vouchernumber": "202611030001",
    "debit": "0.00",
    "credit": "25000.00",
    "runbal": "25000.00"
  }
]
```

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|

| narration | string | No | Description of transaction |
| voucherdate | string | No | Date of ledger entry |
| exchange | string | No | Exchange for the transaction |
| voucherdesc | string | No | Nature of transaction |
| vouchernumber | string | No | Voucher number for the transaction |
| debit | string | No | Debit amount for the entry |
| credit | string | No | Credit amount for the entry |

### Status Codes

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/ledger \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Get LTP

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/marketfeed/ltp

Get last traded price for multiple instruments.

Retrieve LTP for list of instruments with single API request

> **Info:** You can fetch upto 1000 instruments in single API request with rate limit of 1 request per second.

**Before you start with Get LTP API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Add security IDs under each exchange segment. Find them in the [instrument list](/api/v2/instruments).


## Sample request for Get LTP API

Last traded price of Vodafone Idea on NSE (`14366`), its December futures contract on NSE F&O (`58992`) and its BSE equity (`532822`). Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/marketfeed/ltp \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'client-id: <your-client-id>' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "NSE_EQ": [
      14366
    ],
    "NSE_FNO": [
      58992
    ],
    "BSE_EQ": [
      532822
    ]
  }'
```


## Sample response for Get LTP API

Values are illustrative.

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "data": {
    "NSE_EQ": {
      "14366": {
        "last_price": 12.97
      }
    },
    "NSE_FNO": {
      "58992": {
        "last_price": 13.05
      }
    },
    "BSE_EQ": {
      "532822": {
        "last_price": 12.96
      }
    }
  },
  "status": "success"
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#data-api-error) for error codes.


### Request Body Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| NSE_EQ | int-array | No | List of NSE Equity security IDs as integers (comma-separated, e.g. 1333, 11536) |
| NSE_FNO | int-array | No | List of NSE F&O security IDs as integers (comma-separated, e.g. 49081, 49082) |
| BSE_EQ | int-array | No | List of BSE Equity security IDs as integers (comma-separated) |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | LTP data |


### Example Request

```bash
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/marketfeed/ltp \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --header 'client-id: <your-client-id>' \
  --data '{
    "NSE_EQ": [11536],
    "NSE_FNO": [49081, 49082]
  }'
```

## Get Option Chain

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/optionchain

Retrieve real-time Option Chain across exchanges for all underlying. You can fetch OI, Greeks, Volume, LTP, best bid/ask and IV across all strikes.

Retrieve real-time Option Chain across exchanges for all underlying. You can fetch Open Interest (OI), Greeks, Volume, Last Traded Price, Best Bid/Ask and Implied Volatility (IV) across all strikes for any underlying.

**Before you start with Get Option Chain API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Get a valid expiry from [Expiry List](/api/v2/option-chain/get-expiry-list) first. One unique request is allowed every 3 seconds.

## Sample request for Get Option Chain API

NIFTY (`13`) options expiring on 17 November 2026. Use a current expiry from Expiry List. Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/optionchain \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'client-id: <your-client-id>' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "UnderlyingScrip": 13,
    "UnderlyingSeg": "IDX_I",
    "Expiry": "2026-11-17"
  }'
```


## Sample response for Get Option Chain API

Values are illustrative. `oc` has one entry per strike; one strike is shown here.

| UnderlyingSeg | string | No | Exchange and segment of underlying (see Annexure) |
| Expiry | string | No | Expiry date of option in YYYY-MM-DD (active expiries can be fetched from Expiry List API) |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |

## Get Order by Correlation ID

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/external/{correlation-id}

Retrieve order details using your custom correlation ID.

Returns the details and status of an order using the correlation ID you provided when placing it.

**Before you start with Get Order by Correlation ID API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Enter the `correlationId` you sent with the order under Path Parameters below.

## Sample request for Get Order by Correlation ID API

Fetch an order by the `correlationId` you sent when placing it (`dhan-docs-test` here). Replace it and the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/external/dhan-docs-test \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for Get Order by Correlation ID API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "dhanClientId": "1000000132",
  "orderId": "112111182198",
  "correlationId": "dhan-docs-test",

  "transactionType": "BUY",
  "exchangeSegment": "NSE_EQ",
  "productType": "CNC",
  "orderType": "MARKET",
  "validity": "DAY",
  "tradingSymbol": "IDEA",
  "securityId": "14366",
  "quantity": 1,
  "disclosedQuantity": 0,
  "price": 0.0,
  "triggerPrice": 0.0,
  "afterMarketOrder": false,
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Path Parameters

| Parameter | Type | Required | Description |
|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| correlation-id | string | Yes | The user/partner generated id for tracking back. |


### Response Fields

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|-------|------|----------|-------------|
| dhanClientId | string | No | User specific identification generated by Dhan |
| orderId | string | No | Order specific identification generated by Dhan |
| correlation-id | string | No | The user/partner generated id for tracking back. Values: `Max 30 chars`, `Allowed: [^a-zA-Z0-9 _-]`. |
| orderStatus | enum string | No | Last updated status of the order Values: `TRANSIT`, `PENDING`, `REJECTED`, `CANCELLED`, `TRADED`. |
| transactionType | enum string | No | The trading side of transaction Values: `BUY`, `SELL`. |
| exchangeSegment | enum string | No | Exchange Segment see [annexure](/api/v2/annexure#exchange-segment) |
| productType | enum string | No | Product type of trade Values: `CNC`, `INTRADAY`, `MARGIN`, `MTF`. |
| orderType | enum string | No | Order Type Values: `LIMIT`, `MARKET`, `STOP_LOSS`, `STOP_LOSS_MARKET`. |
| validity | enum string | No | Validity of Order Values: `DAY`, `IOC`. |
| tradingSymbol | string | No | Refer Trading Symbol in Tables |
| securityId | string | No | Exchange standard ID for each scrip. refer here |
| quantity | int | No | Number of shares for the order |
| disclosedQuantity | int | No | Number of shares visible |
| price | float | No | Price at which order is placed |
| triggerPrice | float | No | Price at which order is triggered, for SL-M, SL-L, CO & BO |
| afterMarketOrder | boolean | No | The order placed is AMO? |
| boProfitValue | float | No | Bracket Order Target Price change |
| boStopLossValue | float | No | Bracket Order Stop Loss Price change |
| legName | enum string | No | Leg identification in case of BO Values: `ENTRY_LEG`, `TARGET_LEG`, `STOP_LOSS_LEG`. |
| createTime | string | No | Time at which the order is created |
| updateTime | string | No | Time at which the last activity happened |
| exchangeTime | string | No | Time at which order reached at exchange |
| drvExpiryDate | string | No | For F&O, expiry date of contract |
| drvOptionType | enum string | No | Type of Option Values: `CALL`, `PUT`. |
| drvStrikePrice | float | No | For Options, Strike Price |
| omsErrorCode | string | No | Error code in case the order is rejected or failed |
| omsErrorDescription | string | No | Description of error in case the order is rejected or failed |
| algoId | string | No | Exchange Algo ID for Dhan |
| remainingQuantity | integer | No | Quantity pending at the exchange to be traded (quantity - filledQty) |
| averageTradedPrice | integer | No | Average price at which order is traded |
| filledQty | integer | No | Quantity of order traded on Exchange |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Order details |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/external/{correlation-id} \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

---

## Get Order by ID

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/{order-id}

Retrieve details of a specific order by its ID.

Returns the details and status of an order from the order book placed during the day, using its order ID.

If you only have your own tag for the order, use [Get Order by Correlation ID](/api/v2/orders/get-order-by-correlation-id).

**Before you start with Get Order by ID API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Copy the `orderId` from the [order book](/api/v2/orders/get-orders) and enter it under Path Parameters below.

## Sample request for Get Order by ID API

Fetch one order by its ID. Replace the order ID (`112111182198` here) and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders/112111182198 \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


## Sample response for Get Order by ID API

## Get Order Book

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders

Retrieve all orders for the current trading day.

Returns all the orders placed on your account during the day, along with the current status of each one.

For every order you get its details, how much has been filled and at what average price, and when it was placed and last updated. If an order was rejected or failed, the response also tells you why. To look up a single order, use [Get Order by ID](/api/v2/orders/get-order-by-id) or [Get Order by Correlation ID](/api/v2/orders/get-order-by-correlation-id).

**Before you start with Get Order Book API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.

## Sample request for Get Order Book API

Fetch your order book. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/orders \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```

## Get P&L Based Exit

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/pnlExit

Fetch the currently active P&L based exit configuration.

**Before you start with Get P&L Based Exit API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.

## Sample request for Get P&L Based Exit API

Fetch the active P&L exit. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/pnlExit \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```


| Parameter | Data Type | Description |
|-----------|-----------|-------------|
| pnlExitStatus | string | Current status of the P&L exit operation: `ACTIVE` `INACTIVE` |
| profit | float | User-defined target profit amount for the P&L exit |
| loss | float | User-defined target loss amount for the P&L exit |
| enableKillSwitch | boolean | Indicates if the kill switch is enabled for this P&L exit |
| productType | string | Product types applicable: `INTRADAY` `DELIVERY` |

## Sample response for Get P&L Based Exit API

```json title="200 OK"
{
  "pnlExitStatus": "ACTIVE",
  "profit": 1500.0,
  "loss": 500.0,
  "enableKillSwitch": false,
  "productType": [
    "INTRADAY"
  ]
}
```

Check the [Annexure](/api/v2/annexure#trading-api-error) for error codes.


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Current P&L exit config |
| 401 | Unauthorized |


### Example Request

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/pnlExit \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Get Positions

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions

Retrieve all open positions for the current trading day.

Users can retrieve a list of all open positions for the day. This includes all F&O carryforward positions as well.

**Before you start with Get Positions API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.

## Sample request for Get Positions API

Fetch your positions. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```

| unrealizedProfit | float | No | Unbooked Profit and Loss |

| drvOptionType | enum string | No | Option type Values: `CALL`, `PUT`. |

```bash
curl -X GET https://api.dhan.co/v2/positions \
  -H "access-token: <your-access-token>"
```

## Get Full Quote

`POST` https://api.dhan.co/v2/marketfeed/quote

Get full market quote including market depth (5 levels).

Retrieve full details including market depth, OHLC data, Open Interest and Volume along with LTP for specified instruments.

> **Info:** You can fetch upto 1000 instruments in single API request with rate limit of 1 request per second.

**Before you start with Get Full Quote API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Data APIs need an active Data API subscription on your account.
3. Add security IDs under each exchange segment. Find them in the [instrument list](/api/v2/instruments).


## Sample request for Get Full Quote API

Full quote for Vodafone Idea on NSE (`14366`), its December futures contract on NSE F&O (`58992`) and its BSE equity (`532822`). Replace the client ID and access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/marketfeed/quote \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'client-id: <your-client-id>' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --data '{
    "NSE_EQ": [
      14366
    ],
    "NSE_FNO": [
      58992
    ],
    "BSE_EQ": [
      532822
    ]
  }'
```

### Request Body Parameters

|-----------|------|----------|-------------|
| NSE_EQ | int-array | No | List of NSE Equity security IDs as integers (comma-separated, e.g. 1333, 11536) |
| NSE_FNO | int-array | No | List of NSE F&O security IDs as integers (comma-separated, e.g. 49081, 49082) |
| BSE_EQ | int-array | No | List of BSE Equity security IDs as integers (comma-separated) |


### Status Codes

| Code | Description |
|------|-------------|
| 200 | Full quote with depth |


### Example Request

```bash
curl --request POST \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/marketfeed/quote \
  --header 'Accept: application/json' \
  --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>' \
  --header 'client-id: <your-client-id>' \
  --data '{
    "NSE_EQ": [11536]
  }'
```

## Get Super Orders

`GET` https://api.dhan.co/v2/super/orders

Retrieve all super orders for the current trading day.

This API lets you retrieve an array of all super orders placed in a day with their last updated status. This is a special order book which only consists of Super Orders, where the target and stop loss orders are nested under the main entry order leg. Individual legs of each super order can also be found in the main order book with their Order ID.

**Before you start with Get Super Orders API**

1. Generate an access token on [web.dhan.co](https://web.dhan.co) under **Profile → Trading & Data APIs**.
2. Paste it into the `access-token` field below.

## Sample request for Get Super Orders API

Fetch your Super Orders. Replace the access token with your own.

```bash title="cURL"
curl --request GET \
  --url https://api.dhan.co/v2/super/orders \
  --header 'access-token: <your-access-token>'
```

```json title="200 OK"
    "filledQty": 1,
    "legDetails": [
      {
        "legName": "TARGET_LEG",
        "orderStatus": "PENDING",
        "orderType": "LIMIT",
        "quantity": 1,
        "remainingQuantity": 1,
        "price": 14.0,
        "triggerPrice": 0.0,
        "exchangeOrderId": ""
      },
      {
        "legName": "STOP_LOSS_LEG",
        "orderStatus": "PENDING",
        "orderType": "STOP_LOSS_MARKET",
        "quantity": 1,
        "remainingQuantity": 1,
        "price": 0.0,
        "triggerPrice": 12.5,
        "exchangeOrderId": ""
      }
    ]
  }
]
```
